We provide three contributions: 1) a unified evaluation framework testing gradient and loss-based balancing strategies under controlled settings; 2) a theoretical diagnosis explaining why these methods fail, as they conflate fitting speed with discriminative contribution; and 3) a research agenda toward held-out discriminative modality valuation. Experiments on CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI reveal three shortcomings: no strategy reliably outperforms Late Concatenation; performance is sensitive to hyperparameters; and even ratio calibration fails to yield consistent gains. The core issue is fundamental: loss is not utility, and gradients are not importance. Modality imbalance remains unresolved, motivating utility estimation from held-out performance.

</details>


### [107] [Automatic Lyric Transcription for Greek Songs: Scaling and Task Composition Effects in Whisper Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11302)
*Maria Frangiadaki,Dimitrios Damianos,Kosmas Kritsis,Vassilis Katsouros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次针对希腊语自动歌词转录（ALT）开展受控研究，基于Whisper进行模型缩放、多任务学习与两阶段语音到歌唱适配，并构建希腊语ALT基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALT因旋律变化、节奏不规则和伴奏干扰而比语音识别更难，希腊语等低资源语言缺乏ALT基准，亟需系统研究。

Method: 研究Whisper适配：考察模型缩放效应、以转写-翻译比例构成的多任务训练，以及两阶段语音到歌唱适配；并基于GAD，通过人声分离与CTC强制对齐构建片段级对齐歌唱数据集。

Result: 模型缩放持续提升性能；多任务学习主要对小容量模型起到正则化增益；Whisper Large-v3两阶段适配达到27.2%的WER，显著优于零样本基线。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个希腊语ALT基准，验证了缩放、多任务与两阶段适配的有效性，为低资源语言ALT研究提供参考。

Abstract: Automatic Lyric Transcription (ALT) remains substantially more challenging than speech recognition due to melodic variability, rhythmic irregularity, and accompaniment interference. This is heightened in low-resource languages like Greek, where no prior benchmark for ALT exists. We present the first controlled study of Whisper adaptation for Greek ALT, investigating model scaling effects, task composition via multitask training in transcribe-translate ratios, and two-stage speech-to-singing adaptation. We also curate a segment-level aligned singing dataset based on the Greek Audio Dataset (GAD) using source separation and CTC forced alignment. Results show that scaling consistently improves performance, while multitask learning acts as a beneficial regularizer primarily for smaller-capacity models. The 2-stage adaptation in Whisper Large-v3 achieves a Word Error Rate (WER) of 27.2%, a significant improvement over zero-shot baselines, establishing the first Greek ALT benchmark.

</details>


### [108] [MultiHuSE: A Multimodal Dataset for Humour Styles and Emotions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11322)
*Mary Ogbuka Kenneth,Foaad Khosmood,Abbas Edalat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态幽默风格数据集MultiHuSE，含2,407段高清视频、50名演员演绎1,463个文本，覆盖四种心理学幽默风格与中性内容，并部分标注情绪；基线实验显示多模态融合优于单模态（80.1% vs. 77.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 言语幽默的计算识别仍具挑战，需理解语言、表达风格、情绪与文化语境。现有方法多聚焦二分类，且缺乏同时刻画幽默心理学维度与表达变异的数据库。

Method: 构建MultiHuSE多模态数据集：50名人口学多样化演员表演1,463个文本样本，形成2,407段高清视频，涵盖亲和型、攻击型、自我增强型、自我贬低型四种心理学幽默风格及中性内容；部分子集额外标注底层情绪；同一文本由多位演员演绎以捕捉表达多样性。并开展单模态与多模态融合的幽默风格分类基线实验。

Result: 多模态融合在幽默风格分类上优于单模态（准确率80.1% vs. 77.4%），亲和型幽默提升尤为显著（66%→74%）；文本是最强的单一信号，但融合模型仍带来实质改进。

Conclusion: MultiHuSE为连接幽默与情绪的心理学理论提供实证支持，并为人类传播、幸福感及AI驱动交互研究开辟新方向；数据集经最终用户许可协议开放学术使用。

Abstract: Computational recognition of verbal humour remains a challenging task, requiring an understanding of language, delivery style, emotions, and cultural context. Most existing approaches focus on binary classification and lack datasets that capture psychological dimensions of humour alongside variations in expression. We introduce MultiHuSE, a multimodal dataset comprising 2,407 high-definition videos of 50 demographically diverse actors performing 1,463 text samples across four psychological humour styles (affiliative, aggressive, self-enhancing, and self-deprecating), as well as neutral content. A subset is additionally annotated for underlying emotions. The dataset uniquely captures multiple actor interpretations of the same texts, enabling systematic analysis of expressive diversity. Baseline experiments show that multimodal fusion outperforms unimodal approaches (80.1% vs. 77.4% accuracy) in humour style classification, with particularly strong gains for affiliative humour (66% to 74%). While text provides the strongest individual signal, fusion models deliver meaningful improvements. We hope that MultiHuSE provides empirical support for psychological theories linking humour and emotion, while also opening new avenues for research in human communication, well-being, and AI-driven interaction. The dataset is available for academic use under an End-User Licence Agreement.

</details>


### [109] [E-CONAN (Entailment, CONtradition And Neutral) Benchmarks: Arabic Textual Entailment and Natural Inference Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11334)
*Khloud AL Jallad,Nada Ghneim,Ghaida Rebdawi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 E-CONAN 阿拉伯语自然语言推理基准，含 2-way（RTE）与 3-way（NLI）两个数据集，来源多样（自动翻译、人工校验机器翻译、教材人工撰写、新闻标题含谣言），并用于评估 9 个多语言预训练模型、5 个 LLM 及 MARBERT 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语在自然语言推理（NLI）领域资源匮乏，现有基准（如 XNLI、ArNLI）来源单一，难以全面评估模型的泛化能力，因此需要构建来源多样、更具鲁棒性的阿拉伯语推理基准。

Method: 构建 E-CONAN 基准，包含来自四类来源的句对：（1）自动翻译对；（2）人工校验的机器翻译对；（3）阿拉伯语外语教学教材中的人工撰写对；（4）不同新闻频道含谣言的标题对。数据集分为 E-CONAN-2（2 类，RTE）和 E-CONAN-3（3 类，NLI）。使用零样本分类评估 9 个 SOTA 多语言预训练模型，并在 ArNLI、XNLI、E-CONAN 上对比；另评估 5 个 LLM（在 E-CONAN-3 上）及 MARBERT 作为阿拉伯语专用基线，最后进行定性与定量错误分析。

Result: E-CONAN 是评估模型泛化能力、微调预训练模型的有价值资源；其多源混合的构成相较 XNLI 与 ArNLI 提供了更广泛、更鲁棒的评估。同时给出了多语言模型、LLM 与阿拉伯语专用模型（MARBERT）在 E-CONAN 上的性能对比，并归纳出常见错误模式。

Conclusion: E-CONAN 基准将公开，有望丰富阿拉伯语文本蕴含与自然语言推理研究。

Abstract: Natural Language Inference processes pairs of sentences to extract their semantic relations. NLI has been a hot research topic, integrated as a main component in other NLP applications. Despite significant advancements in textual inference across various languages all around the world, Arabic language still suffers from limited resources in this domain. To address this gap, this paper introduces E-CONAN benchmarks that are composed of sentences pairs from various sources: (1) automatically-translated pairs, (2) human-validated machine-translated pairs, (3) hand-crafted pairs from teaching Arabic as foreign language books, and (4) headlines pairs from different news channels containing rumors. E-CONAN contains two benchmark datasets, E-CONAN-2, a 2-way dataset (RTE) and E-CONAN-3, a 3-way dataset (NLI). Additionally, we have used E-CONAN benchmarks to evaluate 9 state-of-the-art multilingual pretrained models using zero-shot classification. Models were evaluated across the ArNLI, XNLI, and E-CONAN datasets. Results show that E-CONAN is a potentially valuable resource for evaluating model generalization and even for fine-tuning pre-trained models. Its diverse composition, derived from a combination of sources, offers a broader and more robust assessment compared to XNLI and ArNLI. In addition, we have evaluated 5 LLMs on E-CONAN-3 dataset. Moreover, we incorporated MARBERT as a representative Arabic-specific baseline and conducted performance evaluation comparison to demonstrate how Arabic-specific models scale against cross-lingual and LLM-based approaches on the E-CONAN benchmarks. Furthermore, we conducted detailed qualitative and quantitative error analysis to analyze frequent error patterns. E-CONAN benchmarks will be publicly available, we hope that it will enrich research community in Arabic textual entailment and natural language inference.

</details>


### [110] [On the Impact of Anonymization on the Performance of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11335)
*Tobias Deußer,Max Hahnbück,Lorenz Sparrenberg,Tobias Uelwer,Christian Bauckhage,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统实证研究了输入数据匿名化对语言模型效用的影响，发现隐私-性能权衡因模型能力和任务类型而异，可逆匿名化优于不可逆方法，且提示模型无益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在敏感领域部署日益广泛，对输入数据进行匿名化以保护个人身份信息已成为关键实践，但匿名化对模型效用的影响尚不明确，隐私与性能的权衡缺乏系统理解。

Method: 对五个主流语言模型、十一个多样化基准进行系统实证评估，对比其在原始输入与伪名化输入上的表现；并进一步比较可逆与不可逆匿名化技术，以及显式提示模型匿名化信息的效果。

Result: 匿名化总体降低性能，但影响高度细微：能力更强的模型（如Qwen2.5-72B、GPT-4o mini）性能下降最大，暗示其更依赖具体实体信息；影响具有任务依赖性，TruthfulQA随匿名化提升，而RGB等检索类任务出现灾难性下降；保持实体唯一性的可逆匿名化显著优于删除等不可逆方法，显式提示模型无可见收益。

Conclusion: 匿名化并非一刀切方案，必须结合模型与任务协同设计以有效平衡隐私与效用；该研究为开发更稳健、隐私感知的AI系统提供了关键基线。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains, anonymizing input data to protect personally identifiable information has become a critical practice. However, the impact of this anonymization on model utility is not well understood. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of the trade-off between privacy and performance. We evaluate five prominent language models across eleven diverse benchmarks, comparing their performance on original versus pseudonymized inputs. Our results reveal that while anonymization generally degrades performance, the effect is highly nuanced. We find that more capable models, such as Qwen2.5-72B and GPT-4o mini, suffer the largest performance drops, suggesting a stronger reliance on specific entity information. The impact is also task-dependent: performance on TruthfulQA improves with anonymization, while retrieval-focused tasks like RGB experience a catastrophic decline. Further experiments show that reversible anonymization techniques that preserve entity uniqueness significantly outperform irreversible ones like redaction, and that explicitly prompting models about anonymization offers no discernible benefit. We conclude that anonymization is not a one-size-fits-all solution and must be co-designed with the model and task in mind to balance privacy and utility effectively. Our findings provide a crucial baseline for developing more robust, privacy-aware AI systems.

</details>


### [111] [SEAR: Segment-Evidence-Aware Routing for Weak-to-Strong Multilingual Speech MCQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11355)
*Huy Hoang Le,Long-Bao Nguyen,Minh Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MLC-SLM Challenge Task 2的参赛系统，基于Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct，通过分段证据感知的数据构建与后训练流水线，在官方评测集上取得90.92%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言对话语音语言模型挑战赛Task 2需要模型理解长时语音中的事件级语义并回答相关问题。现有方法在证据定位、数据质量与音频依赖性训练方面存在不足，因此需要构建证据感知的数据与后训练流程来提升性能。

Method: 以Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct为基座。先用语言模型将带时间戳的ASR转换为连贯事件片段，按边界余量扩展并从原始录音裁剪；再用Qwen3.6-27B生成语义MCQ、Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite生成声学MCQ，经结构、接地、答案一致性及目标模型可训练性检查，得到覆盖21种语言与口音变体的359,825条验证MCQ。通过纯文本探针将数据划分为弱项（文本可答）用于SFT、强项（依赖音频）用于GSPO强化学习，并采用去偏优势、序列级重要性修正与动态过滤进行稳定。

Result: 在最终官方评测集上获得90.92%的准确率。

Conclusion: 证据感知的数据构建、多阶段质量校验与文本/音频分区后训练（SFT+GSPO）能有效提升多语言对话语音语言模型的事件级理解能力。

Abstract: This paper describes our system for Task~2 of the second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge. We adapt Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct with a segment-evidence-aware data and post-training pipeline. A language model converts timestamped ASR into coherent event spans, which are expanded by a boundary margin and cropped from the original recording. We then synthesize complementary semantic MCQs with Qwen3.6-27B and acoustic MCQs with Gemini~3.1 Flash-Lite, followed by structural, grounding, answer-consistency, and target-model trainability checks, yielding 359,825 verified MCQs across 21 language and accent variants. A text-only probe partitions the data into weak, text-answerable items used for supervised fine-tuning and strong, audio-dependent items used for reinforcement learning with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), stabilized by debiased advantages, sequence-level importance correction, and dynamic filtering. Our system obtains 90.92% accuracy on the final official evaluation set.

</details>


### [112] [TransClean: A Benchmark for Detecting and Extracting Clean Translations from Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11399)
*Shenbin Qian,Yves Scherrer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型（LLM）机器翻译输出中普遍存在的“翻译噪声”（如语言标签、解释、双语重复等），基于79万余条输出归纳出12类噪声模式，构建了TransClean基准（9,900对噪声-干净译文），并评估了两种译文提取方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于机器翻译，但其输出常包含译文之外的附加文本（语言标签、解释、双语重复等，即“翻译噪声”）。尽管该问题普遍存在，却缺乏专门的基准与系统性研究。

Method: 分析12个LLM、22个语向对、超过790,000条翻译输出，归纳出12种反复出现的噪声模式，划分为格式噪声与内容噪声两类；据此构建TransClean受控基准，包含9,900对噪声与干净译文（8,800条合成实例 + 1,100条人工整理的真实实例）；在基准上评估两种提取方法：基于跨度的提取（利用翻译质量估计模型进行跨度检测）与基于LLM的提取（提示LLM分离译文）。

Result: 识别出12种反复出现的噪声模式，分为格式与内容两类；构建了规模为9,900对噪声-干净译文的TransClean基准，并在其上对比评估了基于跨度与基于LLM的两种译文提取方法。

Conclusion: 该基准与分析提供了首个用于评估和改进LLM翻译输出“干净度”的系统性框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for machine translation, yet their outputs often contain additional text beyond the translation itself, such as language labels, explanations or bilingual repetitions, which we term translation noise. Despite its prevalence, this problem lacks dedicated benchmarks and systematic study. We analyze over 790,000 translation outputs from 12 LLMs across 22 language pairs (LPs) and identify 12 recurring noise patterns, which we group into formatting and content noise. Building on the observed patterns, we construct TransClean, a controlled benchmark of 9,900 pairs of noisy and clean translation outputs, comprising 8,800 synthetically generated instances and 1,100 manually curated authentic instances. We evaluate two extraction approaches on the TransClean benchmark: 1) a span-based extraction method leveraging translation quality estimation models for span detection, and 2) an LLM-based extraction method that prompts an LLM to isolate the translation. Our benchmark and analysis provide the first systematic framework to evaluate and improve the cleanliness of LLM translation outputs.

</details>


### [113] [SWRouter: Similarity-Contractive Window Routing for Multi-Turn Large Language Model Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11414)
*Yu Wang,Yuchen Li,Rui Kong,Xinran Chen,Jiamin Chen,Hengyi Cai,Shuaiqiang Wang,Jiashu Zhao,Yulun Zhang,Zhonghao Lyu,Haoyi Xiong,Linghe Kong,Jimmy Xiangji Huang,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SWRouter，一种面向多轮对话大模型路由的相似度-收缩窗口路由器，结合基于相似度的上下文分割与双指标评估框架，在多轮对话基准上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由器在单轮场景有效，但难以直接迁移到多轮对话。多轮路由性能关键取决于历史上下文如何被分割、保留并融入当前提示，这带来两个根本挑战：(1) 上下文构建中的信息丢失与信息混淆；(2) 在评估路由质量时无法将模型选择与提示构建质量解耦。

Method: 提出SWRouter（Similarity-Contractive Window Router）。方法包含两部分：一是基于相似度的上下文分割机制用于提示构建，以缓解信息丢失与混淆；二是双指标评估框架，将构建准确性与路由器性能解耦。

Result: 在多轮对话基准上，SWRouter持续超越强基线，相比最佳单一大型语言模型评估准确率提升16.26%，相比Conv-ID Context基线再提升8.22%。

Conclusion: 多轮大模型路由需要上下文构建与评估的联合设计，而非单轮路由方法的直接扩展。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit complementary strengths, motivating routing methods that dispatch each query to the most suitable model. Although existing routers are effective in single-turn settings, they do not directly transfer to multi-turn dialogue, where routing performance critically depends on how historical context is segmented, retained, and incorporated into the current prompt. This introduces two fundamental challenges: preventing information loss and information confusion during context construction, and evaluating routing quality without conflating model selection with prompt construction quality. In this paper, we propose SWRouter, a Similarity-Contractive Window Router for multi-turn large language model routing. SWRouter combines a similarity-based context segmentation mechanism for prompt construction with a dual-metric evaluation framework that decouples construction accuracy from router performance. Experiments on multi-turn dialogue benchmarks demonstrate that SWRouter consistently surpasses strong baselines, achieving a 16.26% improvement in evaluation accuracy over the best individual large language model and an additional 8.22% gain over the Conv-ID Context baseline. Our results highlight that multi-turn large language model routing requires a joint design of context construction and evaluation, rather than a direct extension of single-turn routing methods.

</details>


### [114] [Cross-Lingual Clinical Annotation Projection as Constrained Text Generation: A Six-Language Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11450)
*Álvaro Rey-Blanes,Francisco J. Moreno-Barea,Francisco J. Veredas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将跨语言临床标注投影建模为文本保持的文档级生成任务，通过约束型LLM将实体标签直接插入不可修改的目标语言文本，并结合确定性校验与字符偏移重建；在18个语言-实体组合上取得最高0.9201的平均Strict F1，全面超越此前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言临床语料构建受限于标注数据稀缺与语言专用工具不足，需验证跨语言临床标注投影能否以文本保持的文档级生成方式实现可验证的字符级标注，并系统刻画其鲁棒性与计算开销，以对比候选片段式投影流程。

Method: 提出约束型LLM投影工作流：在不可变目标语言文本中直接插入实体标签，随后进行确定性验证与字符偏移重建。将其与有监督候选片段投影、ML-LLM混合精炼三种方案对比，用于把西班牙语的疾病、症状、操作标注迁移至六种语言。评估采用MultiClinAI金标准，以严格片段匹配和字符重叠F1为指标。

Result: 直接LLM投影性能最强且最稳定：GLM 5.2在18个语言-实体组合上平均Strict F1为0.9201，可本地部署的Gemma4:31B达0.9133。最优LLM配置在全部18种设置上均较此前最优方法提升0.0564–0.1512，共产出55,416条带重建偏移的接地实体提及。

Conclusion: 直接基于LLM的投影可实现高质量多语言临床标注迁移，为向标注数据与语言专用工具稀缺的语言扩展临床NLP资源提供了可行路径；结合本地推理与确定性验证，可显著降低多语言临床语料构建的专家时间与成本。

Abstract: Background: To determine whether cross-lingual clinical annotation projection can be formulated as a text-preserving, document-level generative task that produces verifiable character-level annotations for multilingual clinical corpus construction, and to characterize its robustness and computational trade-offs relative to candidate-based projection pipelines. Methods: We developed a constrained LLM projection workflow that inserts entity tags directly into immutable target-language text, followed by deterministic validation and character-offset reconstruction. We evaluated it alongside supervised candidate-span projection and hybrid ML-LLM refinement for transferring Spanish Disease, Symptom, and Procedure annotations into six languages. Evaluation used MultiClinAI gold standard with strict span matching and character-overlap F1 Results: Direct LLM projection achieved the strongest and most consistent performance. GLM 5.2 obtained a mean Strict F1 of 0.9201 across 18 language-entity combinations, while locally deployable Gemma4:31B achieved 0.9133. The best LLM configuration improved Strict F1 over the previous state of the art in all 18 settings, by 0.0564-0.1512, yielding 55,416 grounded mentions with reconstructed offsets. Conclusions: Direct LLM-based projection enables high-quality multilingual clinical annotation transfer and provides a practical approach for extending clinical NLP resources to languages with fewer annotated datasets and language-specific tools. Combined with local inference and deterministic validation, it can substantially reduce expert time and cost for multilingual clinical corpus construction.

</details>


### [115] [ReGround: Grounding Reviewer Comments in Multimodal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11460)
*Serwar Basch,Lizhen Qu,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ReGround数据集，将10,267条审稿意见链接到3,656篇论文中的16,274条证据，将"审稿意见定位"形式化为检索任务，并揭示该任务的困难性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审稿意见通常针对论文的具体部分，但在长篇幅多模态文档中，将意见与其背后证据建立对应关系十分困难。现有基准未覆盖该场景，且多聚焦于显式、信息检索式的查询，缺乏对审稿意见定位任务的支持。

Method: 构建ReGround大规模数据集，利用作者rebuttal中常显式引用投稿内容这一高精度标注来源进行标注；将证据定位建模为检索任务，并在该数据集上评估多种检索方法。

Result: 在整个论文内容上进行检索表现很差；证据类型推断是主要瓶颈；多模态证据提供了纯文本所缺失的互补信号。

Conclusion: 该数据集表明，审稿意见定位是科学文档理解中一个困难且具有实际重要性的问题。

Abstract: Reviewer comments naturally relate to specific parts of the reviewed paper, yet grounding these comments to the underlying evidence is difficult due to long multimodal documents. Existing benchmarks do not capture this setting and largely focus on explicit, information-seeking queries. We introduce ReGround, a large-scale dataset for reviewer comment grounding that links 10,267 reviewer comments to 16,274 evidence in the original anonymous submission of 3,656 papers. We build on a simple observation: author rebuttals often include explicit references to content of the submission used to address reviewer comments, providing a high-precision annotation source. We cast grounding as a retrieval task and evaluate a wide range of retrieval methods. Results show that retrieval over the entire paper content performs poorly, evidence-type inference is a major bottleneck, and multimodal evidence provides complementary signals that text alone misses. Our dataset exposes grounding reviewer comments as a difficult and practically important problem for scientific document understanding.

</details>


### [116] [Structural priors for data-efficient language learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11505)
*Yana Veitsman,Jonas Mayer Martins,Jonathan Lautenschlager,Lisa Beinborn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究考察"结构迁移"：先用非语言数据（音乐、概率文法、元胞自动机）预训练，再迁移到多语言建模。非语言数据可降低语言建模损失并减少后续权重偏移，但下游语言任务收益不稳定，且效率不及增加语言数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效语言学习需要减少对大规模数据和算力的依赖，因此作者探索能否利用非语言数据来诱导有用的先验，作为多语言语言建模的权重初始化。

Method: 在非语言数据上预训练模型以形成初始化，再迁移到多语言语言建模；通过下一词预测损失、语言训练中的权重变化（weight shifts）以及下游语言基准三方面评估迁移效果，并与随机初始化和增加语言数据两种设定对比。

Result: 音乐、概率文法、元胞自动机等符号数据类型带来的语言建模损失低于随机初始化；这些增益伴随语言训练中更小的权重偏移，说明模型被置于参数空间中更有利的区域。但更低损失并未稳定转化为更好的下游语言表现，且非语言数据迁移的效率低于直接增加语言数据。

Conclusion: 非语言数据可作为下一词预测训练目标的部分替代品，但不能可靠支撑更广泛的语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Efficient language learning requires methods to reduce the reliance on large data and computational resources. We investigate structural transfer: First training models on non-language data to induce useful priors for natural language. This approach is a form of weight initialization for multilingual language modeling. We evaluate transfer via next-token-prediction loss, weight shifts in the model, and downstream linguistic benchmarks. Several symbolic data types - notably music, probabilistic grammars, and cellular automata - yield lower language-modeling loss than random initialization. These gains coincide with smaller weight shifts during subsequent language training, suggesting that structural transfer positions models in a more favorable region of the parameter space. However, a lower loss does not translate consistently into better downstream linguistic performance, and transfer from non-language data is less efficient than additional language data. We conclude that non-language data can serve as a partial substitute for language data for the training objective of next-token prediction but does not reliably support broader linguistic generalization.

</details>


### [117] [Complex-Text Robustness Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis for Low-Resource Multilingual Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11545)
*Tianlun Zuo,Ziyu Zhang,Tingzhi Mao,Zhonghua Fu,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出面向低资源多语言TTS的复杂文本鲁棒性诊断框架，从内容一致性、语言一致性与生成稳定性三方面评测泰语、越南语、斯瓦希里语和印尼语，并引入TRS风险指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低资源多语言TTS评测多聚焦自然度、说话人相似度和常规句内容一致性，对数字、日期、命名实体、长句、语码转换和标点结构等复杂输入的失效模式诊断不足。

Method: 构建三维鲁棒性诊断框架（内容一致性、语言一致性、生成稳定性）；针对泰语、越南语、斯瓦希里语和印尼语设计覆盖普通句与多类复杂文本的多语言测试方案；引入字符错误率、语种识别准确率和时长异常率等自动诊断指标；提出无需人工标注与模型训练的轻量级文本风险分数TRS，基于可解释文本特征进行合成前风险估计。

Result: 在OmniVoice、VoxCPM2和MMS-TTS三个代表性多语言TTS系统上实验表明：复杂文本输入暴露出规则短句评测未能反映的系统性失效模式；不同系统在数字归一化、命名实体处理、长文本生成与语码转换输入上呈现差异化脆弱点；TRS与内容错误和时长异常呈正相关。

Conclusion: 复杂文本鲁棒性诊断框架可揭示低资源多语言TTS的系统性缺陷，TRS可作为低成本、合成前的复杂文本风险指示器，支撑输入级风险分析与多语言TTS鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Low-resource multilingual text-to-speech (TTS) systems have expanded language coverage, but their robustness under complex text inputs remains insufficiently diagnosed. Existing evaluations mainly focus on naturalness, speaker similarity, and content consistency using regular test sentences, while providing limited insight into how multilingual TTS systems fail when handling challenging inputs such as numbers, dates, named entities, long sentences, code-switched expressions, and punctuation-related structures. This paper proposes a complex-text robustness diagnosis framework for low-resource multilingual TTS. We evaluate robustness from three dimensions: content consistency, language consistency, and generation stability. A multilingual robustness testing scheme is designed for Thai, Vietnamese, Swahili, and Indonesian, covering ordinary sentences and multiple types of complex text inputs. We further introduce automatic diagnostic metrics, including character error rate, language identification accuracy, and duration abnormal rate. To support input-level risk analysis before speech generation, we propose a lightweight Text Risk Score (TRS), which estimates synthesis risk from interpretable text features without manual annotation or model training. Experiments on three representative multilingual TTS systems, including OmniVoice, VoxCPM2, and MMS-TTS, show that complex text inputs expose systematic failure patterns that are not fully reflected by ordinary short-sentence evaluation. Different systems exhibit distinct vulnerabilities in number normalization, named entity handling, long-text generation, and code-switched input processing. Furthermore, TRS shows a positive correlation with content errors and duration abnormalities, demonstrating its usefulness as a low-cost pre-synthesis indicator for complex-text risk diagnosis in low-resource multilingual TTS.

</details>


### [118] [A Training-Free, Alignment-Free Approach to Corporate Intelligence: Application to SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11620)
*Jean-François Delpech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于哈希稀疏种子向量的免训练、免对齐框架，将文档与时间窗口统一到公共坐标系，在SEC文件上实现亚秒级、CPU级的可解释企业事件分析，无需LLM推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维稠密文本嵌入与大语言模型在金融信息披露分析中面临上下文窗口限制、幻觉风险、高计算成本，以及独立训练模型间向量空间任意旋转（缺乏共性坐标系）等障碍。

Method: 提出免训练、免对齐的框架，基于确定性稀疏种子向量：将词串哈希到固定高维基，使所有文档与所有时间窗口先天处于同一坐标系；在句子上下文中累积种子向量，得到语料特定的语义签名，并支持线性组合。可执行亚秒级文档比较、发行人指纹、跨文件词汇漂移追踪与主题句抽取，全部在普通CPU上运行。

Result: 在多年期SEC文件（10-K、10-Q、8-K）语料上验证：重大公司事件（如波音737 MAX危机、英特尔供应链中断、邦吉收购Viterra）呈现为可区分的、可解释的语义剖面，且每项均可追溯至产生它的确切源句；无需领域特定训练，也无LLM推理。

Conclusion: 该确定性稀疏种子向量框架为金融披露与企业情报分析提供了一种免训练、免对齐、低成本、可解释且可溯源的替代路径，避免了稠密嵌入与LLM的固有缺陷。

Abstract: High-dimensional dense text embeddings and large language models face real obstacles in financial-disclosure analysis: context-window limits, hallucination risk, high computational cost, and the arbitrary rotation of vector spaces across independently trained models. We present a training-free, alignment-free framework for corporate intelligence built on deterministic sparse seed vectors. Hashing word strings into a fixed high-dimensional basis places all documents and all temporal epochs in a common coordinate system by construction, removing any need for training or alignment. Accumulating these seed vectors across sentence contexts yields corpus-specific semantic signatures that compose linearly, supporting sub-second document comparison, issuer fingerprinting, tracking of how an issuer's vocabulary shifts between filings, and thematic sentence extraction, all on ordinary CPU hardware. Demonstrating the approach on a multi-year corpus of SEC filings (10-K, 10-Q, 8-K), we show how material corporate events, among them Boeing's 737 MAX crisis, Intel's supply-chain disruptions, and Bunge's acquisition of Viterra, emerge as distinct, interpretable semantic profiles, each traceable to the exact source sentences that produced it, with no domain-specific training and no LLM inference.

</details>


### [119] [Structured Transforms for Low-Overhead Quantization of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11687)
*Daria Cherniuk,Alexander Rudikov,Boris Kashin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于Kashin分解的改进权重量化算法，用符号随机化DCT替代稠密随机正交矩阵，将每次迭代开销从O(N²)降至O(N log N)，并消除多起点k-means瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视基于Kashin分解的大语言模型权重量化，旨在获得更强收敛性与结构化高效正交变换，同时保留"权重分解为有界无穷范数分量"的核心思想。

Method: 保留将权重分解为两部分（一个无穷范数有界，另一个经正交变换后无穷范数有界）的核心因式分解，但用符号随机化离散余弦变换（DCT）替代稠密随机正交矩阵；采用交替更新的贪心算法，保证每个因子实现稳定2比特聚类所需的四峰分布，并给出聚类中心的闭式初始化；结合OPTQ式顺序误差补偿与QuIP式不相干预处理构建JAX流水线。

Result: 在OPT、Llama-2和Pythia上，以每通道4比特配置，性能可与OPTQ、QuIP、QuIP-RG及无微调和矢量量化的QuIP#变体竞争，且墙钟时间扩展性更优；在压力配置下（Pythia-6.9B中QuIP变体困惑度发散至四位数、Mistral-7B中LDL回代出现NaN而中止），Kashin-DCT保持数值稳定并接近FP16基线。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持量化竞争力的同时提升效率与数值鲁棒性；推理时每个权重每通道分解为两个2比特因子码，结构上适配原生2比特硬件。

Abstract: We revisit Kashin-decomposition-based weight quantization for large language models and propose an improved algorithm with stronger convergence properties and structured, efficient orthogonal transforms. The method retains the core factorization of each weight into two components -- one with bounded infinity norm and the other with bounded infinity norm after an orthogonal transformation -- but replaces the dense random orthogonal matrix with a sign-randomized Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT), reducing the per-iteration cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(N \log N)$. The proposed greedy algorithm with alternating updates guarantees the four-peak distribution required for stable 2-bit clustering of each factor and admits closed-form initialization of cluster centers, removing the multi-restart k-means bottleneck of prior work. Composed with OPTQ-style sequential error compensation and QuIP-style incoherence preprocessing, the resulting JAX pipeline is competitive with OPTQ, QuIP, QuIP-RG and a fine-tuning- and vector-quantization-free variant of QuIP# at 4-bit per channel on OPT, Llama-2 and Pythia, with favorable wall-clock scaling. The bounded-$\ell_\infty$ factorization is also notably robust: on stress configurations where QuIP variants diverge to four-digit perplexity (Pythia-6.9B) or abort with NaNs in LDL back-substitution (Mistral-7B), Kashin-DCT remains numerically stable and stays close to FP16 baseline. At inference time, each weight decomposes into two 2-bit factor codes per channel that are structurally suited to native-2-bit hardware.

</details>


### [120] [Negative Self-Distillation: Learning to Reason by Avoiding Flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11699)
*Rongcan Pei,Zhepei Wei,Shuyao Xu,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Negative Self-Distillation (NSD)，通过让模型远离自身生成的负面推理轨迹（而非模仿特权解）来优化LLM，并借助动态门控机制隔离推理关键token，避免损害基础语言能力，在复杂推理任务上优于OPSD及无标签自举RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) 让模型充当自身教师、依赖特权信息（如标准答案）来提升LLM，但在复杂推理任务上会严重损害性能：迫使学生在特权信息下模仿人为自信的推理链，会抑制不确定性表达，并惩罚解决难题所需的探索性与自我纠正行为。因此需要一个不依赖特权解、又能避免上述缺陷的新框架。

Method: 提出Negative Self-Distillation (NSD)：不模仿特权解，而是让模型自身生成问题特定的负面条件（如扮演"粗心推理者"），作为负面教师，推动学生分布远离该负面教师，从而实现"从错误推理中偏离"。针对直接套用遗忘目标会导致缺陷推理token与基础语言token混淆、进而灾难性损害语言能力的问题，设计动态门控机制，自动识别并隔离推理关键token，使梯度更新只针对行为缺陷，保留模型的语言先验。

Result: 实验上，NSD在复杂推理任务上持续优于OPSD以及其他无标签、自举式强化学习（RL）基线。

Conclusion: 通过"远离负面推理"而非"模仿特权解"的范式，并配合动态门控隔离推理关键token，NSD在无需外部监督或标准答案的情况下实现了更优的LLM自我改进，为复杂推理场景下的自蒸馏提供了新思路。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a popular paradigm for large language model (LLM) self-improvement, allowing models to act as their own teachers by leveraging privileged information such as ground-truth solutions. However, recent findings indicate that OPSD can severely degrade the performance of LLMs on complex reasoning tasks: By forcing the student to imitate an artificially confident reasoning trace conditioned on privileged information, OPSD inadvertently suppresses expressions of uncertainty and penalizes the exploratory, self-corrective behaviors required to solve challenging problems. To address this, we introduce Negative Self-Distillation (NSD), a new framework that optimizes LLMs by diverging from flawed reasoning rather than imitating privileged solutions. Instead of relying on ground-truth answers or external supervision, NSD uses the model itself to generate a question-specific negative condition (eg, acting as a ``careless reasoner'') and pushes the student's distribution away from this self-generated negative teacher. Naively applying unlearning objectives to achieve this divergence is problematic, as flawed reasoning tokens are confounded with basic linguistic tokens; indiscriminately penalizing both risks catastrophically degrading the model's foundational language capabilities. We resolve this by designing a dynamic gating mechanism that automatically identifies and isolates reasoning-critical tokens, ensuring gradient updates target only behavioral flaws while preserving the model's linguistic priors. Empirically, NSD consistently outperforms OPSD and other label-free, self-bootstrapping reinforcement learning (RL) baselines.

</details>


### [121] [The Eloquence submission for Task 2 of the Interspeech 2026 MLC-SLM challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11724)
*Jordi Luque,Lorenzo Concina,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti,Filippo Vella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍Eloquence团队在Interspeech 2026第二届MLC-SLM挑战赛Task 2中的方案，针对21种语言的多语言选择题问答，探索了三种方法，最优结果macro-accuracy达0.81。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该任务为跨21种语言的多语言选择题问答（MCQA），面临多语言泛化与标签偏置等挑战，目标是超越官方基线并提升整体准确率。

Method: 探索三种方法：（1）对Voxtral-Mini-3B采用LoRA微调，结合跨语言数据增强、ASR转录增强与时间戳感知音频裁剪；（2）对冻结的Voxtral-24B应用多模态上下文学习（ICL）以纠正强标签偏置；（3）基于三层语音锚定记忆（融合声学身份、语义内容与知识图谱）的无训练检索系统。

Result: LoRA微调在评估第二阶段取得0.72 macro-accuracy；多模态ICL达到0.81，为最佳结果；无训练检索系统取得0.68。三种系统均显著优于官方基线。

Conclusion: 多模态ICL在纠正标签偏置方面效果最佳，且无需微调即可取得最高性能；三种方案均一致超越官方基线，验证了多语言MCQA中增强与检索策略的有效性。

Abstract: This paper details the Eloquence team's approach to Task 2 of the 2nd MLC-SLM challenge at Interspeech 2026, which involves multilingual Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) across 21 languages. Three approaches are explored. First, we fine-tune Voxtral-Mini-3B via LoRA with cross-lingual data augmentation, ASR transcript augmentation and timestamp-aware audio cropping, achieving 0.72 macro-accuracy on evaluation Phase 2. Second, we apply multimodal in-context learning (ICL) to the frozen Voxtral-24B model to correct a strong label bias, reaching 0.81, our best result. Third, a training-free retrieval system based on a three-layer voice-anchored memory combining acoustic identity, semantic content, and a knowledge graph achieves 0.68. All three systems substantially outperform the official baseline.

</details>


### [122] [LOCUS: Task-Aware Low-Rank Post-Training for Token-Efficient Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11739)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 LOCUS：通过选择任务感知的低秩适配子空间，在保持偏好对齐目标与冻结主干的前提下，最小化输出 token 成本，从而在不损害效用的条件下显著缩短生成长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型服务成本与输出序列长度直接成正比，而标准偏好对齐常常在没有提升效用的情况下膨胀回复冗长度。因此需要研究后训练更新的参数化方式是否会影响生成长度，并寻找能在效用约束下降低输出 token 成本的方法。

Method: LOCUS 选择任务感知的低秩适配子空间，在该子空间内最小化输出 token 成本，同时受效用约束；后训练在此子空间中保留原生偏好目标，主干网络保持冻结。随后在 Anthropic HH-RLHF 对话偏好数据上，对两个约 3B 解码器主干（Pythia-2.8B 与 Qwen2.5-3B）进行评估，并与协议匹配的全参数 DPO、DrDPO 分支以及已发布的 SamPO checkpoint 进行对比。

Result: LOCUS 在 Pythia-2.8B 上将续写长度最多减少 39.84%，在 Qwen2.5-3B 上减少 14.87–17.58%，且仅更新 0.24–0.28% 的模型参数；内部偏好诊断指标没有实质性变化。

Conclusion: 后训练更新的低秩子空间参数化能够在不改变对齐损失、不显著影响偏好诊断的情况下有效控制生成长度。LOCUS 以极少参数更新实现了显著的输出 token 成本下降，表明参数子空间选择是优化服务成本与效用权衡的有效途径。

Abstract: Large language model serving costs scale directly with output sequence length, yet standard preference alignment often inflates response verbosity without improving utility. We study whether the parameterization of post-training updates affects generation length: low-rank subspaces alter sequence length without modifying the alignment loss. We present LOCUS, a method that selects a task-aware low-rank adaptation subspace to minimize output-token cost subject to a utility constraint. Within this subspace, post-training retains the native preference objective with a frozen backbone. Across Anthropic HH-RLHF dialogue preferences, we evaluate two $\sim$3B decoder backbones, Pythia-2.8B and Qwen2.5-3B, against protocol-matched full-parameter DPO and DrDPO branches and the released SamPO checkpoint. LOCUS reduces continuation length by up to 39.84\% on Pythia-2.8B and by 14.87--17.58\% on Qwen2.5-3B while updating only 0.24--0.28\% of model parameters, with no material change in the internal preference diagnostic.

</details>


### [123] [RAG-Safety-Bench: Reliable Evaluation of Retrieval-Augmented LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11758)
*Adithiyan Rajan Indira Saravanan,Kathleen C. Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAG-Safety-Bench 基准，通过控制检索器质量并分离四种条件，系统测量 RAG 对 LLM 安全性的影响，发现安全能力与良性能力呈反向关系，且基线安全护栏无法保证 RAG 场景下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 可提升 LLM 可靠性并减少幻觉，但已有研究表明 RAG 在被诱导生成有害内容时会对整体安全性产生意外副作用。随着越来越多终端用户将企业文档和知识库接入 LLM 系统，亟需更清晰地理解导致安全性退化的机制。

Method: 提出 RAG-Safety-Bench 基准。通过消除检索器质量的混杂效应，将问题干净地划分为四种条件：非 RAG；RAG 搭配包含有害请求答案的 oracle 文档；RAG 搭配与有害请求相关但无具体答案的文档；RAG 搭配随机安全文档。以此隔离不同因素对安全性退化结果的影响。

Result: 在五个开源 LLM 上进行评测，结果显示：良性能力与不安全能力之间存在反向关系；有力证据表明基线安全护栏并不构成 RAG 场景下的下游安全保证；并支持此前关于即使良性文档也可能导致检索启用系统生成不安全内容的发现，但该效应具有模型特异性。

Conclusion: RAG 对 LLM 安全性的影响是多因素共同作用的结果，且现有安全护栏不足以应对 RAG 场景下的安全风险。RAG-Safety-Bench 为隔离和量化这些因素提供了可控的评测框架，提示在将 RAG 部署于企业文档与知识库时需重新审视安全性保障机制。

Abstract: Allowing large language models (LLMs) to retrieve information from a set of trusted documents can increase reliability and reduce hallucination. However, recent work has demonstrated that retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can have unintended side effects on the overall safety of the generated responses, when prompted for harmful or dangerous content. A clearer understanding of the mechanisms leading to this result is needed, as increasing numbers of end users turn to RAG to incorporate corporate documents and knowledge bases into LLM-based systems. We introduce RAG-Safety-Bench, a benchmark to measure the safety impact of RAG on LLM models. By removing the confounding effect of retriever quality, and cleanly separating the problem into four conditions -- non-RAG, RAG with an oracle document containing the answer to the harmful request, RAG with documents related to the harmful request but without the specific answer, and RAG with random, safe documents -- the benchmark isolates the impacts of different factors in the observed safety degradation. We report results across five open-source LLMs, showing an inverse relationship between benign and unsafe capability, strong evidence that baseline safety guardrails do not lead to downstream safety guarantees in the RAG case, and model-specific support for previous findings that even benign documents can lead to unsafe generation in retrieval-enabled systems.

</details>


### [124] [Component-Aware Differential Privacy for Federated Multilingual Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11762)
*Jordi Luque,Fernando López,Aleix Sant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示逐层差分隐私裁剪在语音大模型上因编码器与解码器更新范数失衡而失效，提出双池分配的 α-split 方法，在不改变整体(ε,δ)-DP保证的前提下恢复效用并增强编码器隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐层DP裁剪按参数量分配裁剪预算以提升联邦学习梯度保真度，但语音大模型（speech-LLM）中声学编码器与语言解码器的更新范数相差一个数量级，导致该方法失效。作者需诊断此"跨组件预算崩溃"的成因，并在保持DP保证不变的前提下修复。

Method: 在六种逐层方法与三种语音大模型架构上实证诊断根因，确认崩溃严重程度与组件间范数比正相关；随后提出 α-split——将编码器与LLM参数归一化到两个独立池中进行双池裁剪预算分配，并通过架构校准的 α 取值实现。

Result: 单池逐层方法出现跨组件预算崩溃，词错误率（WER）远差于扁平全局裁剪甚至训练崩溃；范数失衡较缓时自适应单池方法可部分恢复。α-split 在联合 ℓ2 敏感度与原(ε,δ)-DP保证不变的情况下，于校准 α 下恢复接近扁平DP的WER效用，同时为编码器提供相对高 4.47× 的逐组件噪声保护（针对基于说话人音色的梯度反演攻击），LLM 噪声开销仅增加 2.6%。

Conclusion: 逐层DP裁剪在组件更新范数高度失衡的语音大模型上不可直接套用；按组件划分独立裁剪池的 α-split 能在不牺牲整体DP保证的前提下，同时改善效用与异质隐私保护，为异质模块化模型的分层DP设计提供了可泛化思路。

Abstract: Per-layer differential privacy (DP) clipping improves gradient fidelity in federated learning by allocating per-matrix clipping budgets proportional to parameter count. We show that this recipe breaks for speech large language models (speech-LLMs), when the acoustic encoder and the language decoder differ by an order of magnitude in update norm. Single-pool per-layer methods suffer \emph{cross-component budget collapse}, dragging word error rate (WER) far from flat global clipping or collapsing training entirely. When the norm imbalance is milder, adaptive single-pool methods partially recover, confirming that collapse severity scales with the inter-component norm ratio. We empirically diagnose the root cause across six per-layer methods and three speech-LLM architectures. We then propose \emph{$α$-split}, a two-pool allocation that normalises encoder and LLM parameters into independent pools, and show that joint $\ell_2$ sensitivity and the original $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP guarantee are unchanged. At architecture-calibrated $α$, our method recovers WER utility compared to flat DP, while granting the encoder $4.47{\times}$ tighter per-component noise protection against speaker voice-based gradient-inversion attacks at only $+2.6\%$ LLM noise overhead.

</details>


### [125] [Recognizing Is Not Reversing: A Controlled Inversion Test of Fact-Preserving News Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11769)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出受控反转测试，检验大语言模型能否在保持事实不变的前提下撤销已知的新闻框架变换。在540个配对样本上测试Qwen、DeepSeek和Kimi，发现事实保持率接近0.84，但框架反转率仅为0.044–0.068，即使正确识别框架类型与方向，反转率也仅0.071，表明事实保真、框架识别与框架反转之间存在明显分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架研究主要评估生成、检测或改写文本是否更中立，未能直接检验模型能否在固定事实的条件下撤销一个已知的框架变换。<span>换言之，缺少对"框架反转"能力的直接、可控测量，因而无法判断"识别框架"是否等于"能够逆转框架"。</span>

Method: 提出受控反转测试，覆盖三种既有框架文本实现方式：评价性词汇、施事实现与信息显著性。选取60篇新闻文章，设置三种干预强度，生成540个配对变体，并保持原子事实不变、记录全部编辑。在Qwen、DeepSeek与Kimi上进行评测。

Result: 事实保持率接近0.84，而干预反转率仅为0.044–0.068。即便模型正确识别框架类型与方向，汇总反转率也只有0.071。

Conclusion: 事实保真、框架识别与框架反转三者之间存在清晰分离：识别一篇文章如何被框架化，并不意味着该框架可以被撤销。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to analyze and rewrite news, yet current framing studies mainly evaluate generation, detection, or whether rewritten text appears more neutral. They do not directly show whether a model can undo a known framing transformation while keeping the facts fixed. We introduce a controlled inversion test over three established textual realizations of framing: evaluative lexis, agency realization, and information salience. Across 60 news articles and three intervention strengths, this yields 540 paired variants with preserved atomic facts and recorded edits. Across Qwen, DeepSeek, and Kimi, factual preservation remains near 0.84, whereas intervention reversal is 0.044--0.068. Even when both framing type and direction are recognized correctly, pooled reversal reaches 0.071. These results reveal a clear separation between factual fidelity, framing recognition, and framing inversion: recognizing how an article is framed does not imply that the framing can be undone.

</details>


### [126] [The widening evaluation gap in medical large language model research 2023 to 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11770)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对2023年1月至2026年6月PubMed收录的14个临床领域11,628篇LLM医学研究进行计量分析，发现评估滞后从1.33个季度扩大到6.08个季度，严格的随机对照设计反而更多评估已停用的旧模型，说明严谨性与时效性的矛盾源于模型选择而非研究周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型每隔几个季度就被新一代系统取代，而临床证据的产生需要数年时间。作者由此提出核心问题：医学研究能否跟上它所评估的系统的更新速度？即检验医学AI评估文献的时效性与方法学严谨性之间是否存在系统性脱节。

Method: 检索PubMed 2023年1月至2026年6月共11,628条记录，覆盖14个临床领域；按研究设计（随机对照/前瞻性vs.其他）分类统计。定义"评估滞后"为一项研究最新命名的模型发布时间到该研究自身发表时间的间隔，并追踪其变化趋势。由于停用模型会"机械性"老化，作者构建了一个固定模型构成的反正事实基准来分离漂移来源，计算向新系统迁移对漂移的抵消比例。比较不同研究设计所评估模型的中位年龄，并对仍在开发中的模型做组间比较。

Result: 文献量增长45倍；仅2.5%采用随机、对照或前瞻性设计。评估滞后从1.33个季度扩大到6.08个季度。相对于固定模型构成的反正事实基准，向新系统迁移仅抵消了56%的漂移（95% CI 50-65）。随机试验所评估的模型比其他设计中的模型中位年龄大4.6个季度（P = 3×10⁻¹⁹）；但在命名了仍在开发中的模型的研究中，各设计之间无差异。62%的随机试验评估的是已停用的模型家族。

Conclusion: 严谨性与时效性之间存在张力，但这种张力反映的是模型选择行为而非研究时间线本身——研究者（尤其是开展随机试验者）倾向选择已被验证但已过时的模型，而非等待或采用最新系统。

Abstract: Large language models are superseded every few quarters; clinical evidence takes years. We asked whether medical research is keeping pace with the systems it evaluates. PubMed returned 11,628 records for January 2023 to June 2026 across fourteen clinical domains, growing 45-fold; 2.5% used a randomised, controlled or prospective design. Evaluation lag, from a study's newest named model release to its own publication, widened from 1.33 to 6.08 quarters. Because discontinued models age mechanically, we benchmarked this against a counterfactual holding model composition fixed: migration to newer systems offset only 56% of the drift (95% CI 50-65). Randomised trials evaluated models a median 4.6 quarters older than other designs (P = 3 x 10^-19), yet among studies naming a model still under development no design differed from any other; 62% of randomised trials evaluated a discontinued family. Rigour and currency are in tension, and that tension reflects model selection rather than research timelines.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Word Error Rate: A Switch Aware Evaluation of ASR and Audio Language Models on English Yoruba Code-Switched Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11786)
*Chibuzor Okocha,Christan Earl Grant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对11个现代系统（6个ASR模型、5个音频语言模型）在英语-约鲁巴语码切换语音上进行切换感知评测，发现总体WER掩盖了码切换行为差异：WER最优系统与领先音频LM在WER上无显著差异，但后者在所有切换局部指标上显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统与音频语言模型在单语基准上错误率已很低，但它们在低资源、带变音符号语言中的码切换语音上的表现尚缺乏充分刻画，亟需切换感知的评测方法以揭示聚合指标所掩盖的问题。

Method: 构建确定性的2000句评测集与统一打分流程，对11个系统进行切换感知评测；除WER外，提出并报告切换局部诊断指标：切换进入词错误率（SETER）、窗口化切换点错误率、分语言错误率以及变音符号不敏感WER。

Result: 最佳WER系统（ASR模型）与领先音频LM的WER统计上无显著差异，但音频LM在所有切换局部指标上显著更优；在忠实输出的系统中，约鲁巴语词元识别严重崩溃（几乎所有系统错误率达0.97），英语词元识别显著更好，错误集中在切入约鲁巴语处；若干生成式音频LM无法作为精确转写器，出现翻译、冗长及提示泄露，且强烈依赖提示。

Conclusion: 总体WER会掩盖码切换行为，切换感知的细粒度诊断不可或缺；作者公开manifests、指标实现与评测脚本，以支持面向非洲码切换语音的可复现切换感知基准测试。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems and audio language models (audio LMs) now report low error rates on monolingual benchmarks, but their behavior on code switched speech in low resource, diacritic rich languages remains poorly characterized. We present a switch aware evaluation of eleven modern systems (six ASR models and five audio LMs) on English Yoruba code-switched speech, using a deterministic 2000 utterance evaluation set and a shared scoring pipeline. Beyond word error rate (WER), we report switch localized diagnostics: a switch entry token error rate (SETER), windowed switch point error rates, language specific error rates, and a diacritic insensitive WER. Our central finding is that aggregate WER hides code switching behavior. The best system by WER (an ASR model) is statistically indistinguishable from a leading audio LM on WER, yet the audio LM is significantly better on every switch localized metric. Across faithful systems, Yoruba token recognition collapses (error 0.97 for almost all systems) while English tokens are recognized far better, and errors concentrate sharply at switches into Yoruba. Several generative audio LMs fail as exact transcribers, producing translation, verbosity, and prompt leakage that are strongly prompt dependent. We release manifests, metric implementations, and evaluation scripts to support reproducible, switch aware benchmarking for African code switched speech.

</details>


### [128] [Target leakage, not model class, explains reported accuracy in survey-based cardiovascular screening: a leakage-tiered audit of glass-box and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11838)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif,Samer Ellaham,Cedric Schmitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 心血管筛查模型的"高精度"实为特征集带来的目标泄漏所致；移除两个诊断后特征后所有模型AUROC下降0.049-0.051并压缩至0.0045宽带，模型容量并非瓶颈，评估实践才是约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家健康调查数据训练的心血管筛查模型普遍报告AUROC接近0.89，作者质疑该精度究竟源于真实学习还是目标泄漏，并进一步追问表格基础模型是否改变结论，以及部署所需的多重属性（校准、公平、覆盖、解释、成本）能否在联合审查下同时成立。

Method: 以2022年BRFSS的442,067名受访者的现患心肌梗死为目标，构建五个泄漏风险递减的特征层级，基准测试十种分类器（线性、树集成、神经网络、玻璃盒、表格基础模型）；对每个模型审计区分度、校准、显式筛查阈值下的公平性、保形覆盖、解释忠实度与推理成本；随后将模型与阈值冻结，迁移至2023年430,755名受访者验证。

Result: 移除两个诊断后特征使所有模型损失0.049-0.051 AUROC，模型间差异收敛至0.0045宽带。玻璃盒可解释提升机在预设0.005非劣边界内不劣于任何替代方案，且评分速度约为最强基础模型的104倍。单一阈值对女性心梗检出率75.4%、男性89.0%；编辑模型形状函数后差距缩小至0.010。边际保形预测对男性覆盖0.86、60岁以上成人0.82，Mondrian校准修复了所有层。冻结模型迁移AUROC差异在0.002以内。

Conclusion: 该文献中所谓的精度提升空间是特征集的属性而非学习器的属性；透明度没有带来可测量的代价，反而使公平性修复与不确定性条件化可直接审计。制约因素是评估实践而非模型容量。

Abstract: Cardiovascular screening models trained on national health surveys routinely report areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) near 0.89. We asked whether that accuracy reflects learning or target leakage, whether tabular foundation models change the answer, and whether the properties deployment requires survive joint examination. We benchmarked ten classifiers spanning linear, tree-ensemble, neural, glass-box, and tabular foundation classes for prevalent myocardial infarction in 442,067 respondents of the 2022 Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System across five feature tiers of decreasing leakage risk. Each was audited for discrimination, calibration, fairness at an explicit screening threshold, conformal coverage, explanation faithfulness, and inference cost, then applied -- models and thresholds frozen -- to 430,755 respondents of 2023. Removing two post-diagnostic features cost every model 0.049-0.051 AUROC, collapsing the field into a 0.0045-wide band. The glass-box explainable boosting machine was non-inferior to every alternative within a pre-specified 0.005 margin while scoring the cohort roughly 104 times faster than the strongest foundation model. One threshold detected 75.4% of women's infarctions against 89.0% of men's; editing the model's shape functions reduced the gap to 0.010. Marginal conformal prediction gave 0.86 coverage to men and 0.82 to adults over 60; Mondrian calibration repaired every stratum. Frozen models transported within 0.002 AUROC. Reported headroom in this literature is a property of the feature set, not the learner. Transparency cost nothing measurable and made fairness repair and uncertainty conditioning directly auditable. Evaluation practice, not model capacity, is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [129] [IndicTriMix: Developing Language Identification Datasets and Models for Tri-Language Code-Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11851)
*Pruthwik Mishra,Rudra Trivedi,Avi Patel,Ashok Urlana,Shrikant Malviya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对社交媒体中的语码混合文本，将词级语言识别建模为序列标注任务，微调MuRIL与XLM-RoBERTa模型，在印地语、古吉拉特语和孟加拉语三种数据配置上进行评估，并提出两种基于平行句的语码混合生成方法，同时发布人工标注的基准测试集与微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体中用户常在同一句话内频繁切换多种语言，形成语码混合文本，准确识别其中每个词的语言对后续处理十分必要。而面向单语文本的传统语言识别模型难以适用于语码混合场景下的词级语言识别。

Method: 将任务形式化为序列标注问题，微调适合印度语言的上下文Transformer模型MuRIL和XLM-RoBERTa；在三种数据配置（印地语、古吉拉特语、孟加拉语）上评估，预测每个词的语言标签；提出两种利用三种语言平行句生成语码混合数据的方法。

Result: 所训练模型在词级语言识别上取得良好效果，验证了上下文嵌入在多语言社交媒体文本中词级语言识别的有效性；同时发布了带人工标注测试集的语码混合词级语言识别基准，并公开了微调模型。

Conclusion: 上下文Transformer模型能够有效应对语码混合文本的词级语言识别，所构建的基准与公开模型有助于该任务的可复现性和后续研究。

Abstract: Language identification in code-mixed text, largely observed in social media, is highly essential when users frequently switch between multiple languages within a single utterance. Accurately identifying the languages of code-mixed tokens becomes an urgent necessity. Traditional language identification models, designed for monolingual text, are not well suited for token-level language identification in code-mixed settings. We formulate the task as a sequence labeling problem and fine-tune contextual transformer-based models MuRIL and XLM-RoBERTa best suited for Indian languages. We evaluate these systems on three different data configurations (Hindi, Gujarati, and Bengali) to predict language labels for individual tokens. We release a benchmark for language identification in code-mixed tokens with manually annotated test sets. We propose two approaches of code-mixed generation using parallel sentences of three languages. The trained models demonstrate the effectiveness of contextual embeddings for token-level language identification in multilingual social media text. For reproducibility and to facilitate future research, we publicly release our fine-tuned models.

</details>


### [130] [Epistemic orientation predicts legislative effectiveness among members of the US Congress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11865)
*Segun Aroyehun,Stephan Lewandowsky,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从国会议程层面转向议员个体层面，使用"证据减直觉"(EMI)分数衡量美国国会议员演讲与推文中证据导向语言与直觉导向语言的相对占比，并考察其与意识形态、沟通情境及立法效能的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究显示，自20世纪70年代中期以来美国国会演讲中的证据导向语言整体下降，但这类分析多停留在议程/会话层面，无法回答证据导向倾向是否在议员之间系统性差异，以及是否与其政治行为和立法效能相关。

Method: 采用证据减直觉（EMI）分数，测量国会议员演讲和Twitter帖子中证据导向相对于直觉导向语言的相对普遍程度；将EMI指标与议员层面的意识形态、制度职位、沟通情境及立法效能分数（LES）数据相链接，并进行包含政治、制度、人口、议题和沟通量等多重控制变量的回归分析。

Result: 意识形态越极端的议员在国会演讲中使用证据导向语言越少；EMI在跨平台间具有一致性，即在演讲中更偏证据导向的议员在Twitter上也更偏证据导向，但Twitter整体EMI水平更低；在控制意识形态及大量政治、制度、人口、议题和沟通量变量后，国会演讲中的EMI与议员个人立法效能呈正相关。

Conclusion: 证据导向语言不仅是国会话语的总体特征，也是议员个体层面立法沟通与立法效能的一项有意义属性，说明认识论取向（epistemic orientation）可作为刻画议员行为与效能的个体化指标。

Abstract: Truth and evidence-based communication provide important foundations for democratic governance, accountability, and collective decision-making. Prior work shows that evidence-oriented language in US congressional floor speeches has declined since the mid-1970s, alongside broader changes in legislative productivity and polarization. This study shifts the analysis from congressional sessions to individual members of Congress to examine whether epistemic orientation varies systematically across legislators and whether it relates to political behavior and legislative effectiveness. Using the Evidence-Minus-Intuition (EMI) score, we measure the relative prevalence of evidence-oriented versus intuition-oriented language in congressional floor speeches and Twitter posts. We link these measures to legislator-level data on ideology, institutional position, communication context, and Legislative Effectiveness Score (LES). The results show that more ideologically extreme members use less evidence-oriented language on the congressional floor. EMI also exhibits cross-platform consistency with members who use more evidence-oriented language in floor speeches also being more evidence-oriented on Twitter, although EMI is lower on Twitter overall. Finally, EMI in congressional speeches is positively associated with individual legislative effectiveness, even after accounting for ideology and extensive political, institutional, demographic, topical, and communication volume controls. These findings suggest that evidence-oriented language is not only an aggregate feature of congressional discourse but also a meaningful attribute of individual-level legislative communication and effectiveness.

</details>


### [131] [Augustinian BabyLM: What Ostensive Definition Can and Cannot Teach a Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11870)
*Lisa Bylinina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 视觉初始化在小规模掩码语言模型（DeBERTa，10M词）中留下可测量的持久印记，但在多数BabyLM基准上看不到效果，仅在物体属性知识（COMPS及自建Visual-Property Swap）上体现优势，并可通过合成接地因果迁移；功能词与抽象词也获得强视觉种子却无基准能检出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通常以随机词嵌入开始训练，词义需从语料中习得。作者试图验证St. Augustine式的"以指示义"（ostension）学习图景：若在训练前为视觉接地词元提供源自图像区域的嵌入，是否能改变训练过程与结果，并探究现有评测能否捕捉这种影响。

Method: 对在10M词上训练的小型掩码语言模型（DeBERTa）实施视觉初始化：视觉接地词元的嵌入来自其标注图像区域，其余词元保持随机。使用BabyLM基准测试抽象语法知识，采用COMPS（Misra et al. 2023）测试物体属性知识，并构建语料定制的Visual-Property Swap基准（Lin et al. 2026），覆盖颜色、材料、尺寸、形状知识，且标注每项的训练频率与种子状态。进一步通过为原本未接地的词合成接地做因果检验，并跟踪功能词与抽象词的种子保持情况及留出掩码预测损失。

Result: 视觉初始化留下贯穿训练始终的可测量印记；在多数BabyLM基准上无性能影响；零样本情形下仅物体属性知识（COMPS）在所有配置中受益；在自建Visual-Property Swap上，视觉种子模型呈现可复现的持续优势，且局限于被种子的词；对原本未种子词进行合成接地可将优势精确迁移至这些词；功能词与抽象词同样获得强视觉种子并全程保留，其留出掩码预测损失在每个种子下均下降，但没有任何基准能反映这一效应。

Conclusion: 视觉接地初始化确实改变语言模型训练过程并在特定语义知识上产生可复现优势，且具备因果可控性，但现有评测基准无法捕捉该效应（包括功能词与抽象词上的损失改善），何种评测能检出这一点仍是开放问题。

Abstract: A language model normally begins training with random word embeddings: whatever 'banana' means must be learned from training corpora. I implement St. Augustine's picture of word learning, meaning by ostension, for a small masked language model (DeBERTa) trained on 10M words: before training, visually grounded tokens receive embeddings derived from the image regions they label; other tokens start random. Visual initialization leaves a measurable imprint that lasts until the end of training. At the same time, the effect remains invisible under most BabyLM benchmarks, which probe abstract grammatical knowledge: visual initialization does not affect performance there. The only zero-shot exception is object-property knowledge (COMPS, Misra et al. 2023), where seeding helps in every configuration. To follow up on this result, I build a corpus-tailored version of the Visual-Property Swap benchmark (Lin et al., 2026), which tests color, material, size, and shape knowledge, with per-item training frequency and seeded status. Here, vision-seeded models have a persistent, seed- replicated advantage, confined to the seeded words. As a causal test, I show that synthetic grounding of previously unseeded words transfers the advantage to exactly those words. Function words and abstract vocabulary also receive strong visual seeds and retain them throughout training, and the training objective draws on them: held-out mask-prediction loss falls for these words in every seed. However, no benchmark I run registers this. What evaluation would pick this up remains an open question.

</details>


### [132] [Domain-Specific Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11878)
*Varun Teja Chundru,Debasmita Biswas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多信号检测流水线（DeBERTa-v3微调+MC Dropout不确定性量化+温度缩放校准）用于响应级幻觉检测，在HaluEval上达F1=0.915、AUROC=0.977；并用DPO将生成器幻觉率从85.5%降至37.7%，同时发现通用域训练向生物医学域迁移性差，需领域适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的流畅文本可能包含不忠实的断言，即幻觉现象，影响其可靠性。现有检测手段需要更精确、可解释且能跨任务应用的响应级幻觉识别方法，同时希望通过偏好优化直接降低生成模型的幻觉率。

Method: 构建多信号检测流水线：融合微调DeBERTa-v3分类、Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout不确定性量化与温度缩放校准，进行响应级幻觉检测；在HaluEval基准上评估；开展上下文消融实验与学习曲线分析；对Qwen2.5-0.5B生成器应用Direct Preference Optimization (DPO)降低幻觉；在SciFact生物医学基准上做跨域评估，并对比PubMedBERT领域微调。

Result: 通用域任务上F1=0.915、AUROC=0.977；各任务F1：QA 0.97、摘要0.96、对话0.82；MC Dropout推理将准确率提升至93.2%。上下文消融显示摘要在移除知识上下文后F1下降24%，表明进行真实蕴含推理而非表面模式。学习曲线显示25%训练数据可达全量性能的77%。DPO使生成器幻觉率从85.5%降至37.7%（相对降低55.9%）。跨域SciFact上通用域训练F1仅0.52，PubMedBERT领域微调达F1=0.63、AUROC=0.81。

Conclusion: 多信号检测流水线在通用域幻觉检测上表现强，且通过DPO可显著降低生成模型幻觉率；但通用域训练跨域迁移能力弱，领域匹配的预训练（如PubMedBERT）是最有效的适配策略。代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Large language models generate fluent text that can contain unfaithful claims -- a phenomenon known as hallucination. We present a multi-signal detection pipeline combining fine-tuned DeBERTa-v3 classification, Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout uncertainty quantification, and temperature-scaled calibration for response-level hallucination detection. Evaluated on the HaluEval benchmark, our pipeline achieves F1=0.915 and AUROC=0.977 on general-domain tasks, with per-task F1 scores of 0.97 (QA), 0.96 (Summarization), and 0.82 (Dialogue). MC Dropout inference further improves accuracy to 93.2%. A context ablation study confirms the model performs genuine entailment reasoning rather than exploiting surface patterns, with summarization F1 dropping 24% when knowledge context is removed. Learning curve analysis reveals that 25% of training data captures 77% of full-data performance. Beyond detection, we apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to a Qwen2.5-0.5B generator, reducing its hallucination rate from 85.5% to 37.7% (55.9% relative reduction) as measured by our detector. Cross-domain evaluation on the SciFact biomedical benchmark shows that general-domain training transfers poorly (F1=0.52), motivating domain-specific fine-tuning. PubMedBERT fine-tuned on SciFact achieves F1=0.63 and AUROC=0.81, demonstrating that domain-matched pre-training is the strongest adaptation strategy. Code and models are available at https://github.com/varunteja99/hallucination-detection-nlp

</details>


### [133] [Nuha-Speech: Building General-Purpose Arabic Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11892)
*Yingzhi Wang,Reem Alhazzani,Muhammad Alqurishi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Nuha-Speech，一套面向阿拉伯语语音大模型的综合基础设施，涵盖数据集构建、模型训练与系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语音大模型（speech-LLMs）日益多语言化，阿拉伯语仍严重欠缺代表性，缺乏专门的基础设施来训练与评估阿拉伯语语音大模型。

Method: 构建大规模阿拉伯语语音问答（SQA）语料库，包含超过150万条训练样本，覆盖多种核心语音任务以支持指令微调；基于不同规模的Qwen-Omni模型变体进行监督微调；并设计包含多样化任务与定制化指标的评价框架。

Result: 构建了规模超150万条样本的阿拉伯语SQA语料库，并在不同规模Qwen-Omni模型上完成监督微调，同时建立了多任务评价框架与定制指标。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在在阿拉伯语语音资源受限的条件下，为阿拉伯语语音大模型建立基础性基础设施。

Abstract: As Speech Large Language Models (speech-LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, Arabic remains significantly underrepresented, highlighting the need for dedicated infrastructure to train and evaluate Arabic speech-LLMs.
  To address this gap, we introduce Nuha-Speech, a comprehensive initiative to develop general-purpose Arabic speech-LLMs spanning dataset construction, model training, and systematic evaluation. Specifically, we constructed a large-scale Arabic Speech Question-Answering (SQA) corpus comprising over 1.5 million training samples to allow instruction tuning over a broad range of core speech tasks. Then, the corpus was used for supervised fine-tuning based on Qwen-Omni model variants at different scales. Finally, we designed an evaluation framework featuring diverse tasks and tailored metrics. Through this work, we aim to establish foundational infrastructures for Arabic Speech-LLMs under constraints imposed by limited Arabic speech resources.

</details>


### [134] [Distance generalization in transformers: why bother with positional encoding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11913)
*Daniel Henrik Nevermann,Claudius Gros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究聚焦"距离泛化"：在上下文长度固定的情况下，测试训练与推理时token间距离变化对Transformer的影响，构建两种合成延迟复制任务（全复制与选择性复制），并围绕位置编码方案、数据多样性与迁移学习三问展开系统探究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于分布外长度泛化的研究多关注从短上下文外推到长上下文，而对"距离泛化"——即上下文长度固定、仅token间距离改变时的表现——研究不足。作者希望理解位置编码与训练数据多样性如何影响模型在未见距离上的表现，以及距离迁移学习何时为正、何时为负。

Method: 构建两个合成延迟复制任务（delay copy tasks），均涉及源token与回忆位置之间的有限距离，token要么被完整复制，要么被选择性复制；在训练中未出现的延迟上进行测试。对比不同位置编码方案（RoPE、ALiBi、无位置编码NoPE），并变化训练中可见的token间距离数量（数据多样性），分析距离迁移学习的效果。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值结果，仅提出三个研究问题并声明进行了"全面调查"（thorough investigation）：(A) RoPE与ALiBi相对于NoPE是否提升距离分辨率；(B) 数据多样性（训练中出现的token间距离数量）如何影响性能；(C) 距离迁移学习何时为正、何时为负。

Conclusion: 结论强调：深入理解其背后的机制至关重要（it is paramount to improve our understanding of the underlying mechanisms），即仅凭性能比较不足以解释距离泛化现象，需要进一步揭示内在机理。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution length generalization, namely to extrapolate a task from short to longer context, has been studied intensively for transformers. Here we focus on distance generalization, which probes performance when inter-token distances are changed between training and inference, while keeping a fixed context length. We construct two synthetic delay copy tasks, both involving finite distances between source and recall, where tokens are copied either fully or selectively, and test models on delays unseen during training. We address three questions: (A) Do positional encoding schemes such as RoPE and ALiBi improve distance resolution relative to no positional encoding (NoPE)? (B) How does data diversity, the number of inter-token distances seen in training, affect performance? (C) When is distance transfer learning positive or negative? We present a thorough investigation, finding that it is paramount to improve our understanding of the underlying mechanisms.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Probabilistic Focal Search: Accelerating Bounded-Suboptimal Search via Lower-Bound Advancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10584)
*Minh Vu Duc,Trung Le Huu,Hà Minh Hoàng,Trung Thanh Nguyen,Phuong Khanh Nguyen,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率聚焦搜索（PFS），以概率 p 遵循 FS 引导选择、以 1-p 扩展最小 f 的 OPEN 节点，从而加速下界推进、扩大 FOCAL，缩短求解时间；在 N-Puzzle、TSP 等基准上最多减少约 90% 以上节点扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有界次优搜索需在最优性因子 w 内降低搜索开销。聚焦搜索（FS）在 FOCAL 内做启发式引导，但其确定性策略可能导致 f_min 长期不变，延迟 FOCAL 接纳有用节点，成为搜索瓶颈。

Method: 提出概率聚焦搜索（PFS）：以概率 p 执行 FS 引导选择，以概率 1-p 扩展最小 f 的 OPEN 节点以推动下界上升、扩大 FOCAL。并将同一调度器迁移至 Dynamic Potential Search，得到 PDPS；进一步给出 anytime 扩展 APFS。

Result: 在 N-Puzzle、Pancake Sorting、TSP 上对比 FS，并在 GCTSP 上评估 anytime 扩展，测试多组 w 与 p。当 f_min 出现长平台期延迟 FOCAL 接纳时收益最大，节点扩展可减少约 90% 以上（N-Puzzle、TSP）；APFS 在 GCTSP 的 anytime 评测中优于所有被测算法。Pancake Sorting 等确定性搜索已高效推进的场景收益较小。PDPS 表明机制可迁移，但效果依赖领域与界。

Conclusion: 概率化平衡引导与下界推进可在 FOCAL 接纳成为瓶颈时显著降低搜索努力；该机制对潜在引导亦具可迁移性，但收益受领域和界影响。

Abstract: Bounded-suboptimal search seeks a solution within a factor $w$ of optimal while reducing search effort. Focal Search (FS) uses heuristic guidance within FOCAL, the frontier nodes eligible under the threshold $w f_{\min}$, but its deterministic policy may leave $f_{\min}$ unchanged for many expansions. We introduce Probabilistic Focal Search (PFS), which follows the FS guided choice with probability $p$ and expands a minimum-$f$ OPEN node with probability $1-p$. The latter branch encourages the lower bound to advance, enlarging FOCAL and admitting nodes that may lead to feasible solutions. By balancing guidance and lower-bound advancement, this mechanism can reduce time to a bounded solution when progress is limited by delayed FOCAL admission. As a secondary transfer experiment, we apply the same scheduler to Dynamic Potential Search, yielding Probabilistic Dynamic Potential Search (PDPS). We benchmark PFS against FS on N-Puzzle, Pancake Sorting, and the Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP), and evaluate its anytime extension on the Generalized Covering TSP (GCTSP), using multiple $w$ and $p$ values. Across these benchmarks, the largest gains occur when long $f_{\min}$ plateaus delay useful FOCAL admissions; in such settings, the probabilistic factor may reduce node expansions by about 90\% or more (e.g., on N-Puzzle and TSP). For the anytime algorithm family, Anytime Probabilistic Focal Search (APFS) outperforms all tested algorithms in evaluating anytime methods on GCTSP. We also observe that the benefit is smaller when the deterministic search already advances efficiently (e.g., Pancake Sorting), indicating that the probabilistic factor is most useful when FOCAL admission is a search bottleneck. The PDPS transfer shows that the mechanism also transfers to potential guidance, although its common-success effects remain domain- and bound-dependent.

</details>


### [136] [Automating Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) Formulation Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10629)
*Niloy Kumar Mondal,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端多智能体框架，可自动将自然语言问题描述转化为QUBO形式，并发布QUBOBench基准（12个领域、100个组合优化问题）。框架在基准上达68%准确率，较单次调用基线高22%，迭代自修复是性能提升的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUBO是组合优化的核心形式，因兼容量子、混合量子-经典及量子启发式求解器而备受关注；但从自然语言描述手工构造正确的QUBO（需识别二值变量、约束、目标函数、惩罚项及合适惩罚权重）耗时且高度依赖领域专家，自动化该过程具有实际需求。

Method: 提出端到端多智能体框架，从自然语言问题描述自动生成QUBO形式，并支持结构化或非结构化测试用例；同时构建QUBOBench基准，涵盖12个应用领域的100个组合优化问题，题目取自同行评审文献、竞赛题目及经典NP-hard问题。

Result: 框架在QUBOBench上取得68%准确率，比直接单次调用基线高出22%；消融分析表明迭代自修复（iterative self-repair）是对性能提升贡献最大的模块。

Conclusion: 多智能体+迭代自修复可有效降低自然语言到QUBO的建模门槛，配合QUBOBench提供了可复现的评测基准；数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) is a central formulation for combinatorial optimization and has gained increasing attention due to its compatibility with quantum, hybrid quantum-classical, and quantum-inspired solvers. However, translating natural-language problem descriptions into correct QUBO formulations remains difficult, requiring the identification of binary variables, constraints, objective functions, penalty terms, and suitable penalty weights. This process is time-consuming and often demands substantial domain expertise. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end multi-agent framework that automatically generates QUBO formulations from natural-language problem descriptions, supported by structured or unstructured test cases. To evaluate its performance, We also introduce QUBOBench, a benchmark containing 100 combinatorial optimization problems across 12 application domains, curated from peer-reviewed literature, competitions, and canonical NP-hard problems. Experimental results show that our framework achieves 68% accuracy on QUBOBench, outperforming a direct single-call baseline by 22%. Further analysis identifies iterative self-repair as the most important component contributing to improved performance. The data and code are open-sourced at https://quitttcat.github.io/QuantumQUBOAgent.

</details>


### [137] [A Multi-Stage Rule-Chaining Framework for Compositional and Interpretable Cognitive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10654)
*Deblina Kar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多阶段规则链式框架，通过符号、结构与概念三个层次的组合推理来解决ARC任务，整体准确率超过95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC基准用于评估认知泛化能力，即在有限示例下推断并应用抽象规则的能力；作者希望在不依赖任务特定调优的前提下，推动机器推理走向透明、可解释且与人类对齐的抽象推理。

Method: 框架整合三个互补求解器并顺序执行，形成渐进式回退层级：(1) 确定性规则发现模块，通过几何、颜色和对象分析归纳原子变换；(2) 模式组合引擎，通过块合并、重复和空间启发式重构输出；(3) 结构抽象层，推断网格间的层级与嵌套关系。每个阶段复用先前的推理轨迹以增强可解释性与泛化能力。

Result: 在1000个任务中训练通过995个，在120个任务中进一步评估105个，并在240个ARC-AGI-2测试任务中解决230个；在确定性、组合性与抽象类别上均有较强覆盖，整体准确率超过95%。

Conclusion: 该架构衔接了符号推理与模式合成，为认知泛化提供了可解释的洞见；结果表明规则链式推理与层级组合能够在无需任务特定调优的情况下，推动机器推理向透明、人类对齐的抽象能力发展。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) benchmarks cognitive generalization, the ability to infer and apply abstract rules from limited examples. This paper presents a multi-stage rule-chaining framework that performs compositional reasoning across symbolic, structural, and conceptual levels. The framework integrates three complementary solvers:
  (1) a deterministic rule discovery module that induces atomic transformations through geometric, color, and object-based analysis;
  (2) a pattern-composition engine that reconstructs outputs via block merging, repetition, and spatial heuristics; and
  (3) a structural abstraction layer that infers hierarchical and nested relationships across grids.
  These solvers operate sequentially within a progressive fallback hierarchy, where each stage reuses prior reasoning traces to enhance interpretability and generalization. Training passed for 995 tasks out of 1000, further evaluated on 105 tasks out of 120 and solved 230 test tasks out of 240 ARC-AGI-2 tasks. The system achieved strong coverage across deterministic, compositional, and abstract categories, demonstrating an overall accuracy exceeding 95 percent. The proposed architecture bridges symbolic reasoning and pattern synthesis, providing interpretable insight into cognitive generalization. The results suggest that rule chaining and hierarchical composition can advance machine reasoning toward transparent, human-aligned abstraction without relying on task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [138] [Understanding LoRA Rank Trade-offs in Diffusion Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10656)
*Iman Khazrak,Narges Nejad,Mostafa M. Rezaee,Robert C. Green*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在 CIFAR-10 上以 DDPM U-Net 与 Tiny DiT 为骨干，系统比较 LoRA 秩 {2,4,8,16,32} 对扩散微调质量与计算开销的影响，发现小到中等秩（4–8）最为高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 秩的选择需在微调质量与计算成本之间权衡，但扩散模型微调中缺乏对秩影响的可控对比研究，缺少可复现的实践默认值指导。

Method: 在 CIFAR-10 上使用 DDPM U-Net 做受控实验，秩取 {2,4,8,16,32}，固定优化设置，采用可复现的本地文件夹 pytorch-fid 协议；报告 FID、可训练参数量、运行时间与 GPU 显存；并用延长预算的 DDPM（20 epoch；秩 4/8/16）与 Tiny DiT 骨干（10 epoch；秩 4/8/16）验证趋势。

Result: 中等秩效率最高：秩 4 取得最佳 DDPM FID（124.1380），秩 8 接近（124.2136）；更高秩在适配成本增大时收益有限。

Conclusion: 在固定训练预算下，小到中等秩（约 4–8）可作为扩散微调中 LoRA 的实用默认设置。

Abstract: Selecting LoRA rank for diffusion fine-tuning requires balancing quality and compute cost. We present a controlled study on CIFAR-10 using a DDPM U-Net with ranks {2,4,8,16,32}, fixed optimization settings, and a reproducible local-folder pytorch-fid protocol. We report FID, trainable parameters, runtime, and GPU memory, then validate trends with extended-budget DDPM runs (20 epochs; ranks 4/8/16) and a Tiny DiT backbone (10 epochs; ranks 4/8/16). Results show moderate ranks are most efficient: rank 4 achieves the best DDPM FID (124.1380), rank 8 is close (124.2136), and higher ranks provide limited gains despite larger adaptation cost. These findings support small-to-moderate ranks as practical defaults under fixed training budgets.

</details>


### [139] [Quantifying the Memorization-to-Generalization Transition: Scaling Laws and Phase Structure in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10657)
*Anish Kataria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过384组模运算MLP配置，量化刻画了grokking（记忆到泛化的延迟转变）在超参数空间中的发生时间，拟合出幂律标度关系，并揭示数据复杂度是主导驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于grokking的理论研究多聚焦于“为何”发生，但“何时”在超参数空间中发生、其定量结构仍缺乏系统刻画。作者希望建立可预测、可控制这一机制转变的定量基础。

Method: 在模运算任务上训练两隐藏层MLP，扫描384组超参数配置；绘制记忆-泛化边界；拟合泛化起始时间的幂律标度关系，并考察交互项、相边界与权重范数轨迹。

Result: 拟合得到 T_grok ∝ H^{-0.27} D^{-2.04} η^{-0.50} λ^{-0.64}（R²=0.732，含交互项0.821）。指数层级显示数据复杂度（D^{-2.04}）为主导因素而非模型容量（H^{-0.27}）：数据翻倍加速泛化约4倍，宽度翻倍仅约1.2倍。权重衰减 λ≳1.0 处存在明显相边界，区分grokking与非grokking配置；权重范数在转变期间单调压缩。

Conclusion: 结果为预测与控制过参数化网络中的机制转变提供了定量基础，并支持隐式正则化选择低复杂度解的机制解释。

Abstract: Neural networks trained past memorization frequently undergo a delayed transition to generalization, a phenomenon known as grokking. Despite theoretical progress on \emph{why} this transition occurs, the quantitative structure of \emph{when} it occurs in hyperparameter space remains uncharacterized. We map the memorization-to-generalization boundary across 384 configurations of two-hidden-layer MLPs on modular arithmetic, fitting a power-law scaling relation for generalization onset time: $T_{\mathrm{grok}} \propto H^{-0.27}\, D^{-2.04}\, η^{-0.50}\, λ^{-0.64}$ ($R^2 = 0.732$; $0.821$ with interactions). The exponent hierarchy reveals that data complexity ($D^{-2.04}$) is the dominant driver of regime transition, not model capacity ($H^{-0.27}$): doubling data accelerates generalization by ${\sim}4\times$, while doubling width yields only ${\sim}1.2\times$. A sharp phase boundary at weight decay $λ\gtrsim 1.0$ separates grokking from non-grokking configurations, and weight norm trajectories show monotonic compression during the transition, consistent with implicit regularization selecting low-complexity solutions. These results provide a quantitative foundation for predicting and controlling regime transitions in overparameterized networks.

</details>


### [140] [An Open Recipe for IMO Gold: Training Nemotron for Olympiad Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10712)
*Ivan Moshkov,Stephen Ge,George Armstrong,Wei Du,Sadegh Mahdavi,Igor Gitman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作研究后训练与测试时推理设计对自然语言奥数证明生成的影响，构建了一个纯自然语言、无形式化证明器/外部工具/联网的测试时计算流水线，在IMO 2026上取得42分中的30分，达到金牌线，并开源两个后训练检查点、训练数据与代码、提交解答及新基准Nemotron-IMO-Bench（200道新奥赛级题目）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向高难度奥赛数学的自然语言证明生成极具挑战，而模型后训练策略（SFT、RL）与测试时推理设计（检查点选择、验证、精修）如何影响最终证明质量尚缺乏系统研究。作者希望厘清这些设计因素的影响，并据此构建一个不依赖形式化证明器、外部工具或互联网的开放模型推理系统。

Method: 以Nemotron 3 Ultra为基座，通过监督微调与强化学习训练两个专家检查点；系统评估检查点选择、验证与精修等环节的作用。基于这些发现构建测试时计算流水线：三个Nemotron 3 Ultra检查点（通用模型与两个后训练专家）驱动迭代搜索，生成、验证并精修候选证明；随后由独立的高算力阶段完成最终提交的选择。整个系统仅用自然语言运作。

Result: 该系统在IMO 2026中获得42分中的30分，达到金牌线阈值。

Conclusion: 后训练与测试时推理的协同设计能显著提升自然语言奥数证明能力；作者开源了两个后训练检查点、训练数据、训练与推理代码、提交解答，并发布包含200道新颖奥赛级题目的基准Nemotron-IMO-Bench，以推动相关研究。

Abstract: We study how model post-training and test-time inference design affect natural-language proof generation for hard olympiad mathematics. Starting from Nemotron 3 Ultra, we train two specialist checkpoints using supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, and evaluate checkpoint choice, verification, and refinement. Based on these findings, we present an open-model test-time-compute pipeline. The system operates entirely in natural language, with no formal prover, external tools, or internet access. Three Nemotron 3 Ultra checkpoints - the general-availability model and two post-trained specialists - power an iterative search that generates, verifies, and refines candidate proofs; a separate high-compute stage then selects each final submission. The system scored 30 out of 42 points at IMO 2026, reaching the gold-medal threshold. We release the two post-trained checkpoints as well as the training data, the training and inference code, the submitted solutions, and Nemotron-IMO-Bench, a new benchmark of 200 novel olympiad-level problems.

</details>


### [141] [Finishing the Task Is Not Enough: Evaluating Agent Resilience and Considerate Participation under Accumulating Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10724)
*Yuanchen Bai,Zijian Ding,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"运营韧性"与"体谅性参与"两个评估维度，通过120条模拟医疗轨迹（2个生成式AI模型×12项任务×3档挑战强度）考察挑战累积下智能体的行为与状态报告变化，并据此提炼出五个部署困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续部署生成式AI智能体不能仅以孤立任务成功为衡量标准。智能体需在重复交互、条件变化及依赖他人的共享工作流中保持有用性，尤其当技术、人力与运营干扰随时间累积时。然而现有研究对"挑战累积"情境下的运营韧性与体谅性参与均探讨不足。

Method: 在医疗场景下构建120条模拟轨迹，覆盖2个生成式AI模型与12项源自利益相关者的任务，施加轻度、中度、重度三档挑战；对比三类输出：文本行动计划、提示式内部自评、结构化量化的工作负荷与情绪报告，追踪智能体行为及所报告状态随挑战累积的变化。

Result: 运营韧性方面：智能体从自主恢复转向更多依赖人类，结构化报告中工作负荷与负面情绪上升，但文本回应中极少表达压力。体谅性参与方面：智能体从任务聚焦式适配扩展至任务重构、关注他人、角色边界调整与更广协调，且行动表现与内部自评呈现不同模式。

Conclusion: 基于上述发现提炼出五个部署困境——持续性、注意力、角色边界、状态披露与升级（escalation），需由利益相关者明确规范；并进一步引出关于学习机制、情境化评估与具身适配的技术启示。

Abstract: Sustained deployment of generative AI agents requires more than isolated task success. Agents must remain useful across repeated interactions, changing conditions, and dependencies on people within shared workflows, especially as technical, human, and operational disruptions accumulate over time. We propose operational resilience and considerate participation as two complementary aspects of evaluating such agents: the former captures how agents recover from blocked work while preserving progress and communicating their limits, and the latter captures how their adaptation accounts for affected people, role boundaries, and the surrounding workflow. Yet both remain underexplored under accumulating challenge. We study 120 simulated healthcare trajectories across two generative AI models and twelve stakeholder-derived tasks under light, medium, and heavy challenge. We compare textual action plans, prompted internal assessments, and quantitative structured workload and affect reports to examine how agent behavior and reported state change as challenge accumulates. Regarding operational resilience, agents shift from self-directed recovery toward greater human dependence, while reporting increasing workload and negative affect in structured reports but seldom expressing strain in textual responses. Regarding considerate participation, agents broaden from task-focused adaptation toward task reframing, attention to others, role-boundary adjustment, and wider coordination, with distinct patterns across actions and internal assessments. From these findings, we derive five deployment dilemmas involving persistence, attention, role boundaries, state disclosure, and escalation that require stakeholder specification, further informing technical implications for learning, situated evaluation, and embodied adaptation.

</details>


### [142] [Towards a Deterministic Math Solver for Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10728)
*Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordoñez,Maximin Lange,Rafi Al Attrach,Sahil Kapadia,Zakaria Laouabdia Sellami,Angelo Antonio Talio,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Program-Solve 接口：模型不直接算数，而是生成病例专用的 Python 代码交由受限本地执行器作为确定性求解器运行，从而将模型任务降为"决定如何使用求解器"。在 MedCalc-Bench Verified 上评测 Qwen2.5-7B/32B-AWQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型算术不可靠，而临床计算器中单个数值错误就会改变诊疗建议。常规做法是把每个计算器逐一硬编码为已验证函数，成本高、扩展慢，因此需要测试一种可替代方案。

Method: 以 MedCalc-Bench Verified（1,100 例、55 个计算器）为基准，对照三条路线：模型直接算术、Program-Solve 接口（生成 Python 由受限本地执行器确定性求解）、手工编写的 22 计算器函数库。使用 Qwen2.5-7B 与 Qwen2.5-32B-AWQ；并在评测前将基准公式与现行临床指南核对，标出 55 个公式中 16 个存在版本、用途或系数方面的问题。实验控制为同时提供公式与金标准变量，且两种路线都读取完整病历文本。

Result: 7B 下交接给求解器无可靠增益：75.31% 对 72.02%，配对差 +3.29 个百分点，95% 计算器聚类区间 [-3.49, 10.38]；32B 下有增益：90.53% 对 83.47%，+7.05 [0.47, 14.60]，区间不含零。手工库在其覆盖的 440 例上完全正确，但其余情况弃答，整体仅 40.0%。

Conclusion: 在公式、变量与病历访问条件匹配的情况下，引入执行器对部分开源权重模型的帮助因模型规模而异，并非普适优势；它也不能替代经过验证的公式或可靠的变量抽取。

Abstract: Large language models are unreliable at arithmetic, which is a problem for clinical calculators where a single numerical error changes the recommendation. The standard response is to hardcode each calculator as a validated function, one at a time. We test an alternative: the model does not calculate. Instead, it writes case-specific Python that a restricted local executor runs as a deterministic solver, and the model's task reduces to deciding how to use it.
